# Skills · A · part 101

839 entries, automation-mining to autopilot. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **automation-mining**: Finds repeated manual work in activity records and proposes automations with source evidence. Starts from a team Slack channel, a mailbox, an issue tracker, or supplied work records. The owner reviews each proposal. Use when the user wants automation opportunities found in their own or their team's actual activity: "mine this channel", "what could we automate", "find repeated manual work in these records". Do not use when the user already knows what to build, or wants ideas without granting read access; that is an interview, answer it directly. ([n8n-io/automation-mining-skill](https://github.com/n8n-io/automation-mining-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/automation-mining) · ★46)
- **automation-mode**: Check the status of, follow, or DISABLE VEMO full-auto (unattended) mode. Auto mode stops pausing for user approval and records every decision instead, while keeping all mechanical safety guards and the independent judge. Enabling is NOT available to the agent — a human must run `vemo auto on` in an interactive terminal; this skill covers status, obeying the mode while it is on, and switching it off. ([vemodalen-x/VEMO](https://github.com/vemodalen-x/VEMO/tree/HEAD/skill/automation-mode) · ★1)

- **autonovel-consistency-pass**: Autonovel Phase 3b.1 — cross-chapter consistency review focused on character voice, plot, world rules, and repeated phrasing. Builds a rolling chapter summary then windows over the manuscript in 4-chapter groups, emitting per-window critique and merging into critique/consistency.md. Hands targeted fixes back to autonovel-revision. Max 2 cycles. Designed for the 27B/65k local-model envelope — never loads the full manuscript at once. ([DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills](https://github.com/DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/autonovel-consistency-pass) · ★2)
- **autonovel-drafting**: Autonovel Phase 2 — sequential chapter drafting from the act+scene outline. Each chapter is drafted, gated by mechanical slop detectors, manuscript-repetition scan, and a sentence-grading pass; flagged spans are surgically rewritten in place (see references/surgical-rewrite.md). State.json tracks chapters_drafted / chapters_total and the calibration window. Hands off to autonovel-revision when all chapters complete and the novel-level score has been recorded. ([DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills](https://github.com/DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/autonovel-drafting) · ★2)

([DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills](https://github.com/DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/autonovel-export) · ★2)

Hands off to autonovel-drafting when foundation_score plateaus or threshold met. ([DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills](https://github.com/DukeTwoCan/autonovel-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/autonovel-foundation) · ★2)
